Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The chemical_engineering variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 302 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 180 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to chemical_engineering theory, we decompose the problem into 302 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 180. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
